Saṅ Hyaṅ Hayu

Authors of digital edition: Muhamad Alnoza & Arlo Griffiths

Filename: DHARMA_DiplEdSangHyangHayuIOLJav53t.xml

Language: Old Javanese

Repository: Nusantara Philology (tfd-nusantara-philology)

Version: part commented since without access_token with github actions api calls are limited – still working on it

Witness

  • [IOL_Jav53t] London, British Library, Mss./IOL. Jav. 53t
    • Physical Description: Palm-leaf manuscript. 42 leaves in Javanese script of the Merapi-Merbabu type
    • Hand Description: A1:H1 main hand of the manuscript, black ink, Kawi script
    • History: IOL(Mss.) Jav. 53t is part of MSS. Jav. 53 collections. According to Mackenzie’s report (published in Blagden, 1916), this collection consists of 24 MSS writtten in cadjan leaves. These are said to have been brought by the civility of a regent from a long deserted house in distant forests, where they had lain neglected for many years. The information on IOL Jav. 53z suggests the regent in question was Kyahi Tumenggung Puger. These MSS thus to be collected, probably from the area of Puger in East Java (the ’East Hook’).

    . <history>
    75 <!-- Here you can decribe the history of the manuscript in prose. Use <origDate> and <origPlace> to emphasize the place and date of creation -->
    . <p>IOL(Mss.) Jav. 53t is part of MSS. Jav. 53 collections. According to Mackenzie’s report (published in Blagden, 1916), this collection consists of 24 MSS writtten in cadjan leaves. These are said to have been brought by the civility of a regent from a long deserted house in distant forests, where they had lain neglected for many years. The information on IOL Jav. 53z suggests the regent in question was Kyahi Tumenggung Puger. These MSS thus to be collected, probably from the area of Puger in East Java (the ’East Hook’).
    .</p>
    . </history>
